# Question and targets

What is the complete in-repo list of consumers of the old 32-byte `previewExchangeIn(address,uint256,address)` return (`0x89d61912`)? The required targets are direct and interface-mediated calls, old `uint256` interface bindings, low-level one-word decoders, wrappers, tests, scripts, and frontend bindings. The rate-provider path named at `contracts/vaults/standard/exchange/protocols/uniswap/v4/fullSpread/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN_BLOCKERS.md:196` is explicitly included. Return decoders for `exchangeIn(address,uint256,address,uint256,address,bool,uint256)` (`0xd02d3063`) are separated below.

# Evidence

## Source observations

- The canonical declaration at `lib/crane/contracts/interfaces/IStandardExchangeIn.sol:45-49` returns one `uint256 amountOut` for `previewExchangeIn`; its `exchangeIn` declaration at lines 60-70 also returns one word.
- The required blockers sentence at `contracts/vaults/standard/exchange/protocols/uniswap/v4/fullSpread/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN_BLOCKERS.md:196` says the accepted preview returns `(uint256 amountOut, uint256 amountRefunded)` and names the rate provider as a consumer.
- `StandardExchangeRateProviderFacet` has two old low-level paths: the zero-supply `_initialRate` call at `contracts/protocols/dexes/balancer/v3/rateProviders/standardExchange/StandardExchangeRateProviderFacet.sol:101-105` and `_safePreviewExchangeIn` at lines 115-125. Both require `data.length == 32` and decode `(uint256)`.
- `WrappedStandardExchangeRateProviderTarget._safePreviewExchangeIn` repeats the 32-byte gate and one-word decode at `contracts/protocols/dexes/balancer/v3/rateProviders/standardExchange/wrapped/WrappedStandardExchangeRateProviderTarget.sol:59-69`.
- Three Balancer pool reconciliation paths also use a 32-byte staticcall reply: `CommonBufferMultiVaultStablePoolHookTarget.sol:436-444`, `MixedBufferMultiVaultStablePoolHookTarget.sol:462-470`, and `CommonBufferMultiVaultWeightedPoolHookTarget.sol:473-481` under `contracts/protocols/dexes/balancer/v3/pools/`.
- Local searches used the names `previewExchangeIn` and `exchangeIn`, both literal selectors, signature-string encodings, `staticcall`, `abi.decode`, `.selector`, and frontend ABI/call forms. Source-tree result pages were advanced until `nextSkip=none`.
- Every cited consumer line below was checked against the current file content. Validation found zero missing lines and zero lines lacking the named function.

## Classification rule (inference)

A high-level Solidity call is a consumer even when its value is ignored, because the declared ABI still decodes one word on success. A low-level call-data construction is included only when source context shows a one-word result decode directly or through a wrapper. Selector registration, selector-hash checks, call-expectation-only sites, revert-only calls, gas-only calls, implementation-only declarations, prose, and ignored build output are not counted as consumers. Rows aggregate every verified line for one path and kind; no line represents an unexamined match.

# Assumptions

- “In-repo” means the working tree's source and tracked documentation, not independent consumers duplicated in gitignored generated output. `out/`, `artifacts/`, `typechain/`, and cache trees are derived mirrors; they must be regenerated from migrated source rather than treated as separate source-of-truth rows.
- Historical evidence patches, archived source copies, PRDs, and other prose are not live executable consumers. Their mentions were examined but are not mixed into the source inventory.
- The disposition “migrate” identifies the old return decoding only. It does not prescribe how any product should use `amountRefunded`.

# Coverage

- Complete source searches covered `contracts/`, `test/`, `scripts/`, `frontend/`, `lib/crane/contracts/`, `lib/crane/src/`, `lib/crane/test/`, `lib/crane/scripts/`, `lib/crane/certora/`, `lib/crane/utils/`, `lib/crane/lib/`, `lib/crane/broadcast/`, `lib/crane/snapshots/`, and `lib/ethskills/`.
- Additional repository trees checked for names and selectors included root configuration, `docs/`, `research/`, `prds/`, `deployments/`, `marketing/`, `notes/`, `implementation-artifacts/`, `images/`, `broadcast/`, `tmp/`, and repository instruction/skill directories. Remaining hits were documentation or historical evidence, not additional live source consumers.
- No-name/no-selector source hits occurred in `lib/crane/src`, `lib/crane/test`, `lib/crane/certora`, `lib/crane/lib`, `lib/crane/broadcast`, `lib/crane/snapshots`, `lib/ethskills`, `deployments`, `marketing`, `broadcast`, `tmp`, and the searched root configuration files. Patterns: `previewExchangeIn|0x89d61912` and `exchangeIn|0xd02d3063`.
- Final inventory: 1077 preview consumer lines in 408 files, aggregated into 423 rows; 2190 `exchangeIn` decoder lines in 564 files, aggregated into 599 rows.

## Examined and unaffected families

| Family | Search pattern | Path searched | Cited example | Why unaffected |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Exact-out preview | `previewExchangeOut\s*\(` | `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `lib/crane/contracts/interfaces/IStandardExchangeOut.sol:51` | Different function and exact-out semantics; not the old `previewExchangeIn` return. |
| Exact-out execution | `(?<![A-Za-z0-9_])exchangeOut\s*\(` | `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `lib/crane/contracts/interfaces/IStandardExchangeOut.sol:67` | `exchangeOut` is explicitly outside this return change. |
| Many-to-one exact-in preview | `previewExchangeInManyToOne\s*\(` | `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `contracts/interfaces/IStandardExchangeInMulti.sol:14` | A different multi-asset function, not `previewExchangeIn(address,uint256,address)`. |
| Many-to-one exact-in execution | `exchangeInManyToOne\s*\(` | `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `contracts/interfaces/IStandardExchangeInMulti.sol:31` | A different multi-asset function, not scalar `exchangeIn`. |
| One-to-many exact-out preview/execution | `previewExchangeOutOneToMany\s*\(` | `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `contracts/interfaces/IStandardExchangeOutMulti.sol:14` | Different output-array surface; neither scalar preview nor scalar execution. |
| Non-Standard-Exchange hook previews | `preview(Swap|Deposit|Withdraw|Mint|Burn|Join|Exit|Bond|Redeem|Zap|Claim)[A-Za-z0-9_]*\s*\(` | `contracts/hooks`, `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `contracts/hooks/uniswap/v4/interfaces/IUniswapV4SeBufferHook.sol:38` | Hook-specific previews such as `previewSwapExactIn` do not decode the Standard Exchange preview return by virtue of their names; only their explicit `previewExchangeIn` calls are listed above. |
| Salt previews | `previewSalt|saltPreview|preview[A-Za-z0-9_]*Salt|Salt[A-Za-z0-9_]*preview` | `contracts`, `test`, `scripts`, `frontend`, `lib/crane/contracts` | `contracts/hooks/uniswap/v4/factory/UniswapV4HookDiamondPackageCallBackFactory.sol:97`; `frontend/apps/indexedex/app/create/lib/hookMine.ts:27` | CREATE2 salt prediction has no Standard Exchange return decoding. |

# Limits

This is a static source inventory, not an implementation plan. No Forge, tests, installs, deployments, wallets, or Git commands were run. It does not authorize implementation or a PRD edit. F13 and F14 are not reopened.
